+            // Withdraw Mode Toggle & Step Progress Helpers
+            const btnWitToggleCrypto = document.getElementById('btnWithdrawToggleCrypto');
+            const btnWitToggleMoney = document.getElementById('btnWithdrawToggleMoney');
+            const withdrawMainTitle = document.getElementById('withdrawMainTitle');
+            const withdrawCryptoStepsContainer = document.getElementById('withdrawCryptoStepsContainer');
+            const withdrawMoneyStepsContainer = document.getElementById('withdrawMoneyStepsContainer');
+
+            window.setStepWithdrawMode = function(mode) {
+                if (mode === 'money' || mode === 'inr') {
+                    if (btnWitToggleCrypto) btnWitToggleCrypto.classList.remove('is-active');
+                    if (btnWitToggleMoney) btnWitToggleMoney.classList.add('is-active');
+                    if (withdrawMainTitle) withdrawMainTitle.textContent = 'Withdraw money';
+                    if (withdrawCryptoStepsContainer) withdrawCryptoStepsContainer.style.display = 'none';
+                    if (withdrawMoneyStepsContainer) withdrawMoneyStepsContainer.style.display = 'block';
+                    if (typeof window.switchCurrencyTab === 'function') {
+                        window.switchCurrencyTab('withdraw', 'inr');
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    if (btnWitToggleMoney) btnWitToggleMoney.classList.remove('is-active');
+                    if (btnWitToggleCrypto) btnWitToggleCrypto.classList.add('is-active');
+                    if (withdrawMainTitle) withdrawMainTitle.textContent = 'Withdraw crypto';
+                    if (withdrawMoneyStepsContainer) withdrawMoneyStepsContainer.style.display = 'none';
+                    if (withdrawCryptoStepsContainer) withdrawCryptoStepsContainer.style.display = 'block';
+                    if (typeof window.switchCurrencyTab === 'function') {
+                        window.switchCurrencyTab('withdraw', 'usdt');
+                    }
+                }
+            };
+
+            if (btnWitToggleCrypto) btnWitToggleCrypto.addEventListener('click', () => window.setStepWithdrawMode('crypto'));
+            if (btnWitToggleMoney) btnWitToggleMoney.addEventListener('click', () => window.setStepWithdrawMode('money'));
+
+            const withdrawNetworkSelect = document.getElementById('withdrawNetworkSelect');
+            const withdrawWalletAddress = document.getElementById('withdrawWalletAddress');
+            const withdrawAmountInput = document.querySelector('#withdrawForm input[name="amount"]');
+
+            function updateWithdrawStepProgress() {
+                const step1Circle = document.getElementById('withdrawStepCircle1');
+                const step1Line = document.getElementById('withdrawStepLine1');
+                const step2Circle = document.getElementById('withdrawStepCircle2');
+                const step2Line = document.getElementById('withdrawStepLine2');
+                const step3Circle = document.getElementById('withdrawStepCircle3');
+                const step3Line = document.getElementById('withdrawStepLine3');
+                const step4Circle = document.getElementById('withdrawStepCircle4');
+                const step4Line = document.getElementById('withdrawStepLine4');
+                const step5Circle = document.getElementById('withdrawStepCircle5');
+
+                const netVal = withdrawNetworkSelect ? withdrawNetworkSelect.value : '';
+                const addrVal = withdrawWalletAddress ? withdrawWalletAddress.value.trim() : '';
+                const amtVal = withdrawAmountInput ? parseFloat(withdrawAmountInput.value) : 0;
+
+                if (netVal) {
+                    if (step1Circle) { step1Circle.classList.add('is-completed'); step1Circle.innerHTML = '✓'; }
+                    if (step1Line) step1Line.classList.add('is-active');
+                    if (step2Circle) { step2Circle.classList.remove('is-inactive'); step2Circle.classList.add('is-active'); }
+                } else {
+                    if (step1Circle) { step1Circle.classList.remove('is-completed'); step1Circle.innerHTML = '1'; }
+                    if (step1Line) step1Line.classList.remove('is-active');
+                }
+
+                if (addrVal) {
+                    if (step2Circle) { step2Circle.classList.add('is-completed'); step2Circle.innerHTML = '✓'; }
+                    if (step2Line) step2Line.classList.add('is-active');
+                    if (step3Circle) { step3Circle.classList.remove('is-inactive'); step3Circle.classList.add('is-active'); }
+                } else {
+                    if (step2Circle && netVal) { step2Circle.classList.remove('is-completed'); step2Circle.innerHTML = '2'; }
+                    if (step2Line) step2Line.classList.remove('is-active');
+                }
+
+                if (amtVal > 0) {
+                    if (step3Circle) { step3Circle.classList.add('is-completed'); step3Circle.innerHTML = '✓'; }
+                    if (step3Line) step3Line.classList.add('is-active');
+                    if (step4Circle) { step4Circle.classList.remove('is-inactive'); step4Circle.classList.add('is-active'); }
+                    if (step4Line) step4Line.classList.add('is-active');
+                    if (step5Circle) { step5Circle.classList.remove('is-inactive'); step5Circle.classList.add('is-active'); }
+                } else {
+                    if (step3Circle && addrVal) { step3Circle.classList.remove('is-completed'); step3Circle.innerHTML = '3'; }
+                    if (step3Line) step3Line.classList.remove('is-active');
+                }
+            }
+
+            withdrawNetworkSelect?.addEventListener('change', updateWithdrawStepProgress);
+            withdrawWalletAddress?.addEventListener('input', updateWithdrawStepProgress);
+            withdrawAmountInput?.addEventListener('input', updateWithdrawStepProgress);

7384:             // Helper to prevent wheel scrolling from bubbling to body/lenis
7385:             function isolateDropdownScroll(el) {
7386:                 if (!el) return;
7387:                 el.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
7388:                     e.stopPropagation();
7389:                     const delta = e.deltaY;
7390:                     const up = delta < 0;
7391:                     const atTop = el.scrollTop <= 0;
7392:                     const atBottom = el.scrollTop + el.clientHeight >= el.scrollHeight - 1;
7393: 
7394:                     if ((up && atTop) || (!up && atBottom)) {
7395:                         e.preventDefault();
7396:                     }
7397:                 }, { passive: false });
7398: 
7399:                 el.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {
7400:                     e.stopPropagation();
7401:                 }, { passive: true });
7402:             }

7385:             function isolateDropdownScroll(el) {
7386:                 if (!el) return;
7387:                 el.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
7388:                     e.stopPropagation();
7389:                     const delta = e.deltaY;
7390:                     const up = delta < 0;
7391:                     const atTop = el.scrollTop <= 0;
7392:                     const atBottom = el.scrollTop + el.clientHeight >= el.scrollHeight - 1;
7393: 
7394:                     if ((up && atTop) || (!up && atBottom)) {
7395:                         e.preventDefault();
7396:                     }
7397:                 }, { passive: false });
7398: 
7399:                 el.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {
7400:                     e.stopPropagation();
7401:                 }, { passive: true });

=== NEW SNIPPET ===

Withdrawal mode toggle (**Crypto** | **Money**) નો પ્રોબ્લેમ સફળતાપૂર્વક સોલ્વ કરી દેવામાં આવ્યો છે!

### 🛠️ સોલ્વ કરવામાં આવેલા પોઈન્ટ્સ:

1. **Instant Mode Switching**:
   - `Crypto` અને `Money` પીલ બટન પર ક્લિક કરવાથી હવે તુરંત વિથડ્રોઅલ મોડ અને તેને સંબંધિત 5-સ્ટેપ ફોર્મ કન્ટેનર્સ (`#withdrawCryptoStepsContainer` / `#withdrawMoneyStepsContainer`) પર્ફેક્ટલી સ્વિચ થાય છે.

2. **Page Refresh Persistence (સેસન સ્મૂથ રિફ્રેશ)**:
   - પેજ રિફ્રેશ કર્યા પછી પણ યુઝરે સિલેક્ટ કરેલો વિથડ્રોઅલ મોડ (`Crypto` અથવા `Money`) બદલાશે નહીં અને તે `sessionStorage` તથા URL query parameter દ્વારા યાદ રહેશે.

3. **Global Event Delegation**:
   - બટન ક્લિક અને ટેબ સ્વિચિંગ લોજિકને રોબસ્ટ બનાવી દેવામાં આવ્યું છે જેથી કોઈપણ સંજોગોમાં ટોગલ અટકે નહીં.

Asset build (`npm run build`) 0 errors સાથે કમ્પાઈલ થઈ ગયું છે.

7376:             function toggleWithdrawCurrencyDropdown(open) {
7377:                 if (!withdrawCurrencyDropdownPanel) return;
7378:                 const shouldOpen = open !== undefined ? open : (withdrawCurrencyDropdownPanel.style.display === 'none');
7379:                 withdrawCurrencyDropdownPanel.style.display = shouldOpen ? 'block' : 'none';
7380:                 if (withdrawCurrencyDropdownChevron) {
7381:                     withdrawCurrencyDropdownChevron.style.transform = shouldOpen ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0deg)';
7382:                 }
7383:                 if (btnOpenWithdrawCurrencyDropdown) {
7384:                     btnOpenWithdrawCurrencyDropdown.classList.toggle('is-open', shouldOpen);
7385:                 }
7386:                 if (shouldOpen) {
7387:                     if (withdrawCurrencySearchInput) withdrawCurrencySearchInput.value = '';
7388:                     renderWithdrawCurrencyDropdownList('');
7389:                     setTimeout(() => withdrawCurrencySearchInput?.focus(), 60);
7390:                 }
7391:             }
7392:

7395:                     e.stopPropagation();
